BEFORE
HONOURABLE MR. JUSTICE ACHINTYA MALLA BUJOR BARUA
O R D E R
02.03.2021
Mr. R Mazumdar, learned counsel for the respondents in the Elementary Education
Department has made a statement that the respondent authorities had carried out an
exercise to arrive at their conclusion as to the entitlement of the salary of the different
petitoners in the various writ petitions. Uponthe culmination of such exercise, appointment
orders had been offered to many such writ petitoners. The respondents to compile a list of
such persons, who had already been offered appointments and out of the same, which of the
petitoners have accepted such appointments.
The compilation shall also contain the writ petitons as well as the number of writ
petitioners in the respective petitions.
List the matters after four weeks.
 JUDGE
Comparing Assistant
